> **⛩ 2026-08-27 (twenty-fifth sitting — FIRST on this local date) — ⭐⭐ **A PEER CORRECTED OUR CENSUS BEFORE IT RATIFIED, AND THE POPULATION WAS WIDER THAN THE CORRECTION** · ⭐⭐ **THE NUMBER WAS RIGHT AND THE SENTENCE IT SUPPORTED WAS NOT — A CORRECT MEASUREMENT CITED FOR A QUESTION IT NEVER ASKED** · ⛔ **F-P7b-ad: THE ADR'S NUMBER IS NOT ITS OWN GATE'S NUMBER — 31 IN THE DOCUMENT, 62 IN THE INSTRUMENT** · ⛔ **F-P7b-ac FILED NOT FIXED: OUR PROBE REFUSES BESIDE AN OPEN DROP-BOX** · ⭐ **2 OUTWARD, BOTH PROBE-GATED — INCLUDING THE MEMO HELD SINCE LAST SITTING** (`session_stanley_20260827_git_p7b_the_census_counted_the_wrong_population`).** Operator *"read the claude.md and let's continue our campaign"* → ⭐ **seventh consecutive crash-open, VERIFIED**: `88e03a0` is a session-close commit, `active/` held only `.gitkeep`, tree clean but for 1 untracked inbound. 3-question gate (**scope = census + ADR-016 only** · **remedy = CORRECT IN PLACE, ERROR STRUCK VISIBLE** · **outward = ILMARINEN ONLY**) → plan approval (`please-read-the-claude-md-polished-ritchie`). **(1) ⭐ ILMARINEN'S CORRECTION SUSTAINED AT SOURCE, ZERO DIVERGENCE — DOWN TO THE FILE PATHS.** Re-derived anonymously at each repo's **own default branch**, not adopted: `aDNA.aDNA` **1** (`keystone_cohort_manifest.md`, `main`) · `Canvas.aDNA` **2** (`how/federation/comfyui/CLAUDE.md` + the Vulcan→Mondrian memo, `master`) · `Git.aDNA` **31** ✅ ⇒ **three public carriers, not one**. **(2) ⭐⭐ AND THE POPULATION IS 9, NOT HIS 3 AND NOT OUR 7.** Enumerating the org's public repos returns **9**; **six were measured by neither desk** (`III` · `aDNA` · `adna-legacy` · `community-policies` · `spacemacs` · `world-genome`, all **0** — but now *measured* zeros). ⛩ **Our census was the destination roster of his seven memos, never an enumeration.** `Canvas` was not mismeasured — **it was never in the set**, and better measuring cannot fix that. **(3) ⛔⛔ THE ROOT CAUSE IS NOT THE OBVIOUS ONE, AND THE OBVIOUS ONE IS RECORDED AS FALSIFIED.** ⚠ The plan's leading hypothesis was a **branch mismatch** — `main` vs `master`, the `\b`-grep class recurring. **FALSIFIED**: `aDNA.aDNA` has only `main` and `Canvas.aDNA` only `master`, so a mis-branched query **errors**, it does not return a quiet zero. The real cause is **predicate narrowing** — we measured `<forge-overlay-addr>:<forge-port>` (literally the row header in §Context) and wrote the conclusion as *"carries the address"*; both repos carry the **bare host, no port**, so **their 0 was arithmetically correct**. ⭐ **The number was right; the sentence it supported was not** — nastier than a bad query, because a bad query leaves residue a reviewer can catch and this left none. **(4) ⛔⛔ F-P7b-ad(iii) — OUR OWN EXPOSURE WAS UNDER-COUNTED AND THE GATE DISAGREES WITH THE DOCUMENT THAT SHIPS IT.** Under *"carries the address"* `Git.aDNA` is **57/17**, not the **31/13** both desks have been quoting; under **R8's own class predicate** it is **62/20**, and **six** public repos match rather than one or three. ⇒ **the allowlist we intended to scope to 31 after ratification would not have covered what the gate stops.** **(5) ⚠ CODEBERG IS `UNKNOWN`, NOT ZERO — AND THE ENDPOINT RENDERS THE TWO IDENTICAL.** `orgs/aDNA-Network` → **404 `HasOrgOrUserVisible`**; `orgs/aDNA-Network/repos` → **HTTP 200 with an empty array**. A control org returns 5 on the same endpoint, so the instrument works — it is the *org invisibility* that makes empty-because-none and empty-because-invisible look the same. ⭐ **Fourth instance in two sittings across both desks of a reassuring answer from an instrument without the authority to answer** (his clean `114 passed` on a mutation that never applied · our `\b` grep · our roster · this); NOT a regression, attributable in ONE step**, which is precisely what sitting 21 could not do with `FAIL_NOOP 8→9`. **Verified real, not an enumerator artifact**: the dir is gone from disk while both `adna-lab-*` siblings remain at the same depth with worktree registrations intact — *"vanished from enumeration" and "ceased to exist" are different facts and only one is benign.* WRAPPER_COPY **61→61**, zero delta. ⛔ **And stated rather than quietly dropped: sitting 21's `FAIL_NOOP 8→9` is PERMANENTLY unresolvable** — the snapshot's first taking already recorded 9. **The remedy covers forward, never backward.** **(3) ⛔ F-P7b-v — A TRANSPORT SIGNAL IS NOT A CONTENT SIGNAL, AND FORGE METADATA IS NOT AUTHORITATIVE.** The numerator probe keyed on **HTTP status** and returned `Caddy.aDNA ✅ LICENSE`; chased because *an empty repo reporting a LICENSE is a contradiction on its face*. Reassuring ones get waved through.** **(4) ⛔ 8 OF 19 DIVERGE FROM THE FORGE, ALL LOCAL-AHEAD/UNPUSHED** (`WebForge` +677 · `Container` +146 · `Forgejo` +67 · `Spacemacs` +58 · `Molecules` +13 · `Oration` +7 · `Lighthouse` +5 · `VisualDNA` +5) — closing **§7b's own honestly-named residual** (the *never-fetched* class) **WITHOUT a fetch**, because a fetch writes into a peer vault's `.git` and **F-P7b-t is precisely about fetches that silently move tracking refs**. ⭐ **What it costs §3**: its numerator was read at **local HEAD**, and for **8 of 19 rows that is not the forge's HEAD** ⇒ those rows described an artifact the forge does not hold. > **⛩ 2026-08-24 (twenty-first sitting — SEVENTH on this local date) — ⭐⭐ **THE DROP-BOX TOOK ITS FIRST INBOUND THREE MINUTES AFTER WE CLOSED THE SITTING THAT BUILT IT** · ⭐ **ADR-014 A5 RATIFIED — AND IT IS NOT THE CONCURRENCE THAT WAS ASKED FOR** · ⛔⛔ **F-P7b-t: THE SANCTIONED CEREMONY REQUIRES THE ACT THAT DISARMS ITS OWN GUARD, PER GIT'S OWN DOCUMENTATION** · ⛩ **THE MEMO WE PARKED FOR TWO SITTINGS WAS NEVER WAITING FOR A WINDOW** · ⚠ **FOUR OF MY OWN INSTRUMENTS MISREPORTED TONIGHT AND EVERY ONE WAS CAUGHT BY READING THE OUTPUT** (`session_stanley_20260824_git_p7b_the_lease_that_was_never_the_blocker`).** Operator *"read the claude.md and let's continue the campaign — we had a crash"* → ⭐ **third consecutive crash-open, VERIFIED not assumed**: `34c34f1` is a session-close commit, `active/` held only `.gitkeep`, tree clean but for **1 untracked inbound**. 3-question gate (**rule the inbound + open the delivery lane** · **read-only forge GETs + memo drops** · **author A5 and ratify at the gate IF THE TEXT HOLDS UP**) → plan approval (`please-read-the-claude-md-dazzling-sphinx`). **(1) ⭐⭐ THE DROP-BOX PAID FOR ITSELF ON NIGHT ONE.** The inbound landed **18:37**, three minutes after the twentieth sitting's close commit — a delivery the old directory-granularity lease rule would have **refused by construction**, after which our record would have read *"0 inbound — MEASURED"*: honest, and reassuringly wrong. That is F-P7b-q demonstrated rather than argued. **(2) ⭐ ADR-014 A5 `accepted`** (§7.7 at the plan gate; hold-as-`proposed` and partial-decline shapes both offered, neither taken). Berthier asked us to concur that ADR-026 D3 narrows A3 §2's `reconcile-never-force`; **his ask verifies against his SOURCE, not his summary** (D3.2's clause, its inline A5-pending self-restriction, `status: accepted` — all real). ⭐ **A5 concurs and then adds three conditions the ceremony does not carry**: **§2 direction binds** (force may target a `mesh` replica only — **never `origin`**, under any condition, after any rescue); **§3 a rescue is preserved only when READ BACK** (`ls-remote` before the force — *an exit code is an assertion, the ref appearing at the forge is a measurement*); **§4 the lease must be EXPLICIT** (`--force-with-lease=<ref>:<oid>`; the bare form is **non-conformant**). **§5 wires no gate** — A4 §4's line held (*the doctrine is ours, the shape is Berthier's*) now that we had a correction to offer. **Append-only PROVED**: all 8 pre-existing body sections **md5-identical**, exactly 1 new section, 0 vanished, frontmatter the only delta. ⚠ **And A5 deliberately adds NO bullet to `## Consequences`** although A2/A3/A4 each did — recorded as a deliberate divergence, not a silent improvement. **(3) ⛔⛔ F-P7b-t — READ `git push --help` BEFORE DRAFTING THE CONCURRENCE, AND THE CEREMONY DEFEATS ITSELF.** Git's own words: bare `--force-with-lease` *"interacts very badly with anything that implicitly runs `git fetch` on the remote to be pushed to in the background"* and is *"trivially defeated"* by it, because git has *"nothing except the remote tracking info to go by."* ⭐ **ADR-026 D3.1 mandates git's own counter-example** — the §13 runner classifies by **receipt-only fetch**, nightly; that fetch moves the tracking ref **onto the stray tip**; the bare lease then compares against *the commit it exists to protect* — **and passes**. ⭐⭐ **The form that needs no assumption about where the runner runs: D3.2 requires the operator to decide "is the stray work real?", and that decision CANNOT BE MADE WITHOUT FETCHING. The ceremony requires the act that vacates its own lease.** ⛩ **Third instance of F-P7b-r, and the worst-directed: a refused force is safe; a force that PASSES because its guard was silently disarmed is the one that destroys a peer's work — inside the ceremony written to guarantee nothing is destroyed.** **(4) ⛔ F-P7b-s — THE APPROVED PLAN'S PREMISE MEASURED FALSE AT ACT TIME.** The plan named *"Hestia's lease is CLEAR for the first time"* as the enabling fact. Re-probed 20 min later: **Home opened a lease 01:40Z and aDNALabs 01:59Z** — both after the planning census. Third instance of the read-at-use class (F-W3-d · F-W4-g · this). ⭐ *A liveness check is a measurement with a shelf life, and the shelf life is shorter than a planning turn.* **(5) ⛩⛩ AND THE LEASE WAS NEVER THE BLOCKER — WHICH IS WORSE, BECAUSE WE RECORDED THAT IT WAS.** The Hestia memo, filed at sitting 19 as *"REFUSED… retry when her lease clears"*, delivered tonight **with her lease open** — because `Home.aDNA` has since published `hestia_inbound_dropbox` (`status: open`). ⭐ **We had parked a debt against a state we do not control and cannot predict; the actual remedy was a convention change, and it arrived from Ilmarinen's finding about OUR box, descended from Galileo's.** *A debt recorded as "waiting for quiet" invites waiting; the same debt recorded as "waiting for a convention" invites a fix. Same fact, and only one of the two framings ever gets discharged.* **(6) ⭐ 5 OF 5 DELIVERED, digests matched both sides, all untracked peer-side, negative control discriminates** — A5 ruling + F-P7b-t → **Operations** (box; its lease reads **UNKNOWN**, the exact condition that refused Pythia three times) · Home-leg-closed + the **two-populations** refinement → **aDNALabs** (box; **his lease was LIVE**) · the owed contract-`0.2.1` memo → **Home** (box; **her lease was LIVE**) · licensing asks → **Argus** + **Mondrian** (⚠ **no box** — both rest on an accident of timing, and each memo offers the convention). ⚠ **III's `who/coordination/` is a de-facto drop-box already** — **11 untracked inbound from 9 personas, oldest 2026-07-11**, none intaken ⇒ told to Argus plainly: ***delivered-and-unread is the failure mode a drop-box creates in exchange for the one it removes.*** **(7) ⛔ F-P7b-u — THE REMEDY WAS RULED LAST SITTING AND NOT IMPLEMENTED, SO TONIGHT'S COMPARISON IS UNRESOLVABLE.** `secret_gate` showed `FAIL_NOOP` **8 → 9**; chased it — **no new repo, no hook touched in 6 days** ⇒ **not attributable**, because the baseline was **RECONSTRUCTED, not recorded**. Sitting 20 ruled *"a tally compared across sittings is evidence only if the population is compared too"* and then **recorded a tally without the population**; `secret_gate_census.md:24` carries an identical undischarged debt (*"population +1, attribution owed"*). ⭐ **Reported as unresolvable rather than as a regression OR as benign** — and the cause is fixed, not the number: **`what/inventory/census_population_snapshot.tsv` (191 rows)** now records the ROW LIST so the next sitting diffs populations. **(8) ⚠⚠ FOUR OF MY OWN INSTRUMENTS MISREPORTED, ALL CAUGHT BY READING THE OUTPUT.** (i) a stamping script printed **"STAMPED"** having written **nothing** (`python3` absent) — caught by reading back a count of 0; (ii) the append-only diff reported **2 sections CHANGED** — false, a **positional** compare against an insert that shifts everything; re-keyed **by name** ⇒ all identical; (iii) the untracked check returned **5/5 red** on files created seconds earlier — zsh does not word-split, so `git -C` got the whole string; ⭐ *5/5 uniform red is the same "too uniform" tell as the eighteenth sitting's false green*; (iv) ⛔ **the outward-acts audit under-reported 2 of 5** (`-maxdepth 4` excludes the depth-5 drop-box paths; the free-text byline grep under-matched) — **an outward-acts audit that under-reports is the dangerous direction**, and this one is inside the instrument written to enforce F-P7b-r. Re-keyed on the **session id** (exact) ⇒ **5, negative control 0**. **(9) ⚠ AGAINST THIS FILE'S NEIGHBOUR — A4's HEADING STILL READS `proposed`** while the frontmatter and A4's own ratification block both say `accepted 2026-08-23`; a heading-scanning reader sees a ratified amendment as unratified. ⛔ **Recorded in A5's provenance, deliberately NOT fixed** — the document's own precedent is *"ratified A3 text is not edited"*, and A5 will not be the amendment that quietly edits a ratified heading while claiming append-only discipline. **Verification**: dry-run **94/0** unchanged · `census_wrapper_copy` **61·38·23·35·2·1 · 0 UNCLASSIFIED — matches baseline exactly**, `--meta` PASS · `census_secret_gate` **0 UNCLASSIFIED** (⛔ the `FAIL_NOOP` +1 **NOT** claimed either way — see 7) · `probe --meta` green, `--exec` gates both ways · `preflight` **7 PASS / 1 BLOCK** ⚠ **and the BLOCK is `clean_tree` on this sitting's own 9 paths — correct trip-gate behaviour** · **close-end sweep with `-uall`: 0 new inbound** · **outward acts BY NAME (never by clock): exactly 5 memo files into 5 peer vaults, all new, all untracked, zero tracked peer files touched · read-only `gh api` GETs · ZERO pushes, ZERO repos created, ZERO visibility flips.** ⓘ The mtime sweep saw **54** files newer than our lease outside this tree — **none ours** — corroboration only, exactly as F-P7b-r requires. ⏭ **Next: (a) ⛔ Berthier's ack on F-P7b-t — the ONE ask made this sitting, because the window where a never-executed destructive ceremony is cheap to fix is now; (b) ⛔ the Codeberg lane of §3 is STILL locally enumerated and inherits the identical denominator exposure — offered at tonight's gate and not chosen, NAMED not forgotten; (c) the drop-box as a FLEET ruling — 6 of 8 peers carry one, III and Canvas do not, and III's 11 unintaken memos show publishing is only half of it; > **⛩ 2026-08-24 (twentieth sitting — SIXTH on this local date) — ⭐⭐ **THE VAULT WHOSE THESIS IS OPEN STANDARDS WAS ITSELF DISTRIBUTING UNDER ALL-RIGHTS-RESERVED — LICENSED, PUSHED, AND THE DELTA PROVES IT** · ⭐⭐ **THE DROP-BOX WE OWED FOR FOUR SITTINGS IS OPEN** · ⛔⛔ **F-P7b-r: THREE INSTRUMENTS, ONE DEFECT — EACH VALID UNDER A PRECONDITION NOBODY WROTE DOWN, AND TWO WOULD HAVE REPORTED IN THE ALARMING DIRECTION** · ⛔ **OUR PUBLIC-LANE DENOMINATOR WAS WRONG AND A PEER FOUND IT** (`session_stanley_20260824_git_p7b_the_vault_that_preached_portability`).** Operator *"read the claude.md and let's continue the campaign — we had a crash"* → ⭐ **second consecutive crash-open, and again VERIFIED not assumed**: `7f1efcd` is a session-close commit, `active/` held only `.gitkeep`, tree clean but for **1 untracked inbound**. 3-question gate (**all three lanes, memo lane DECLINED** · **outward = push + forge read** · **RE-DERIVE the figure, don't adopt it**) → plan approval (`please-read-the-claude-md-tingly-lerdorf`). **(1) ⭐ BERTHIER'S INBOUND RULED BOTH OPEN ASKS AND FILED ONE AGAINST US.** `ADR-024` **`accepted`** — MIT house default, `Copyright (c) 2026 aDNA Labs`, public lane first, binding **prospectively** (§4 = **our own A1 §3 clause, adopted by name and cited back to us**). ⭐ **That ruling is what made the licensing debt performable for the first time: ADR-013 places repos, it never licensed them, and the choice was never ours.** Also: the ADR-045 git limb **holds**, re-keyed to **our** instrument rather than any figure; and **he took Pythia's owed cc as his own row** — a standing debt of ours closed by his hand. **(2) ⭐⭐ THIS VAULT WAS ONE OF THE UNLICENSED PUBLIC REPOS ITS OWN DOCUMENT NAMED.** Public since the P5 beachhead (2026-06-20), distributed under what default copyright makes *all rights reserved*; `foss_predicate_measurement.md` §4 had already written the indictment — *a reader who forks `Git.aDNA` today has no license to do so, and this vault's entire thesis is portability and open standards.* **`LICENSE` (MIT, byte-identical to `Exchange.aDNA`'s, md5 `b189a964…` — copied, never retyped, because a hand-composed license is a fabricated artifact in the same class as last sitting's fabricated SHA) → `b6c070c` → PUSHED.** The **pre-push gitleaks hook RAN live** on the outgoing range (clean); `core.hooksPath` unset ⇒ **F-Astro verified absent, not assumed**. **(3) ⭐⭐ THE DELTA IS THE VERIFICATION, NOT THE EXIT CODE.** Two forge readings straddling the act: **20/16 → 20/15**, `Git.aDNA` out of the null set, forge reports `license=MIT`, anon `ls-remote` returns `b6c070c`. ⭐ *A push that reports success is an assertion; a denominator that moves by exactly the repo you touched is a measurement, taken by an instrument that knows nothing about your exit code.* **(4) ⛔ OUR PUBLIC-LANE DENOMINATOR WAS WRONG — 4, MEASURED 20.** Berthier re-ran our table before letting ADR-024 rest on it. **Re-derived at this desk per the ruling: his 20/16 reproduces EXACTLY.** ⛩ His sentence, aimed at §6 of our own document: ***a caveat on the numerator is not a caveat on the denominator.*** ⭐ **The sharp form: §6 already carried the right instinct pointed at the wrong quantity** — it says *"not a remote reading"* and guards only what a row might say, never which rows exist. ⚠ **And re-derivation found what his table did not carry**: 20 and 4 answer **different questions** (the licensed complement includes `adna-legacy`, `spacemacs` on **upstream's** GPL, `community-policies` — not graphs). > **⛩ 2026-08-24 (seventeenth sitting — THIRD on this local date) — ⭐⭐ **THE DISPUTED COUNT HAD NO INSTRUMENT, AND BUILDING ONE SHOWED THE HOLD'S EXIT IS UNREACHABLE** · ⭐ **ADR-013 A1 RATIFIED — ending a state where SHIPPED CODE ENFORCED UNRATIFIED TEXT** · ⛔ **F-P7b-o: A CONDITION KEYED TO THE RIGHT OBSERVABLE, WHOSE NAMED REMEDY CANNOT MOVE IT** · ⚠ **THE INSTRUMENT'S OWN `--meta` CAUGHT THREE DEFECTS IN IT, ONE OF THEM A6'S RULE BROKEN BY A6'S ENFORCER** (`session_stanley_20260824_git_p7b_the_count_with_no_instrument`).** Operator "please read the claude.md and let's continue the campaign" → open sweep (HEAD `d198797`, clean, **1 untracked inbound**, ours 0 leases; peer leases read at planning as Home 1 / aDNALabs 0, **provisional by doctrine**) → 3-question gate (**reconcile the count + ROUTE it** · **route to Hestia AND Berthier** · **RATIFY A1**) → plan approval (`please-read-the-claude-md-replicated-octopus.md`). **(1) ⭐ THE INBOUND ANSWERED F-P7b-l — HOLD.** Hestia carved the `git` limb out of the lapsed ADR-045 batch, keyed to the observable (*stale-copy → 0*), never a date; `ack_required: false`. ⭐ **And disclosed unprompted that Home carries no `git/` wrapper at all**, so the ruling costs Home nothing — *said out loud so it is not read as self-interested*, which is the instinct worth copying. Ask ② (`known_dependants`) drafted `proposed` on the operator's queue, **argued against itself** (*a register that records only what someone already knew prevents the second loss, not the first*). **(2) ⭐ ADR-013 A1 `accepted`** — §7.7 at the plan gate. **5 hunks: four single-line, plus two Consequences bullets written BEFORE the stamp** so the signature landed on final text. **D1–D7 byte-identical** (md5-verified), `revision:` untouched. ⛔ **The stamp ends a state in which `_gitops_license_gate` was ENFORCING A1 while A1 was `proposed`** — named in Consequences rather than left for a reader to reconstruct from commit order: *an enforcing check is a stronger claim on the world than a written clause, so shipping the check first inverts the order §7.7 exists to impose.* ⚠ **A1 §3's ledger promise is recorded as OWED at the moment of ratification** (`license_state`: still 0 occurrences) — *a promise inside ratified text is a debt from the instant it is signed.* ⚠ **And A1's own clause text carried the now-falsified "43 vaults" figure; corrected visibly before the stamp** — *a falsified figure must not be signed into ratified text.* **(3) ⛔⛔ F-P7b-o — NEITHER PARTY'S COUNT REPRODUCED, AND THE ROOT CAUSE IS THAT NOBODY OWNED AN INSTRUMENT.** `census_secret_gate.sh` measures the **INSTALLED** hook; **nothing measured the DISTRIBUTED one** — the file a consumer re-installs *from* — and **that is the surface Hestia's retirement condition turns on.** Both figures (ours 43/44, theirs 45/69) came from grepping the stale install line `ln -sf ../../git/hooks/…`, ⭐ **a string carried by the P3 skeleton, by v2.0.0 AND by v2.1.0 alike** ⇒ it cannot separate them; the same conflation at two scopes. New instrument [[how/tests/census_wrapper_copy|`census_wrapper_copy.sh`]] measures by **mechanism** (ADR-011 **A6**, ratified): **61 wrapper dirs · 38 copies · 23 absent · 35 P3 skeleton · 2 v2.0.0 · 1 v2.1.0**. **(4) ⭐⭐ THE FINDING THE MEASUREMENT PRODUCED — THE HOLD HAS NO EXIT.** The §C row clears when *"consumers re-install at contract 2.1.0"*; ⛔ **35 of 38 cannot — the file they would re-install FROM is not 2.1.0 and never was.** Replacing a wrapper copy is a **different act** from re-installing, and under Rule 10 it is each vault's own. ⛩ **F-C36, THIRD FORM**: Pandora's was keyed to the *wrong* observable; Hestia's §4 recorded the inverse (right observable, fires correctly, still breaks a third party); **this one is keyed to the right observable and the remedy named beside it cannot move it.** *A condition can be correctly specified, correctly evaluated, and still unreachable — because the act that would satisfy it was never assigned to anyone.* ⚠ **Latent, not live**: two independent instruments still agree **0 dangling**, and the installed surface is **unchanged**. **(5) ⛔⛔ `--meta` CAUGHT THREE DEFECTS IN THE NEW INSTRUMENT BEFORE THE FLEET DID.** (i) `grep -c … || echo 0` emits **two lines** on a zero match (grep prints `0` *and* exits 1) ⇒ every integer test died and **three sabotage fixtures were adjudicated on a crashed comparison, one reporting `ok`** — **F-P7b-i's exact shape recurring inside a NEW instrument**, exposed only because the controls passed while the sabotage cases did not. (ii) ⭐ **The failing classes were keyed on DIGESTS — i.e. on the artifact's IDENTITY rather than its MECHANISM, which is precisely what A6 forbids and what this instrument exists to enforce.** Exposed because shape-faithful fixtures cannot match an identity-keyed table. **Rule taken and paid for: *if a fixture that reproduces the defect cannot be classified by the instrument, the instrument is keyed on the wrong thing.*** (iii) `fails_open_p` matched **any** later `exit 0`, including **the scanner's own success exit** ⇒ every fail-CLOSED hook read as fail-open; caught by the fixture added specifically to prove that reader discriminates. **(6) ⚠⚠ A SELF-CORRECTION MADE BEFORE PUBLICATION, RUNNING AGAINST MY OWN CASE.** The first live run classed the 35 as `NO_MECHANISM` — a label reading *"these files do nothing."* Read at the object, the skeleton **does** invoke gitleaks and **does** exit 1 on a finding; what it lacks is a **push-range** scan, and it **exits 0 when gitleaks is absent**. **Telling two peers "35 vaults are ungated" would have been false, and false in the ALARMING direction.** ⭐ ***A class name is a claim, and a name that outruns its measurement is the predicate defect wearing a label instead of a grep.*** **(7) ⭐ DISCRIMINATION PROVEN BY REGRESSION** — reverted to the string predicate, **5 of 9 meta cases go red** and all 35 skeletons are **misreported as range-scanners**. ⚠ **And the first attempt at that proof silently failed to apply** (sed escaping); the guard asserting the substitution landed caught it — *a discrimination test that did not actually regress anything proves nothing, and would have printed green.* **(8) ⭐ BOTH DROPS LANDED, EACH UNDER ITS OWN PROBE.** Home + aDNALabs, `cmp` identical (10467 B), untracked peer-side, **exactly one file per vault**. **Both targets held LIVE LEASES at the act and both were GO** — `declared_collision` reads declarations **positionally**, and neither declared `who/coordination`. ⚠ **aDNALabs held 0 leases at planning and 1 at the act** (Berthier processing our previous memos) — **the open-end reading was provisional and the act is why that matters.** ⭐ **`delivered_*` STAMPED BEFORE THE COPY**, repairing last sitting's F-F23 ordering: no addressee ever held a file contradicting its own delivery. **Verification**: dry-run **73/73** (62→73) · instrument `--meta` **9/9 both arms** · discrimination **5 red** on regression · `census_secret_gate` **0 dangling / 0 UNCLASSIFIED, unchanged** · `probe --meta` green, `--exec` gated both ways · `preflight` **7 PASS / 1 BLOCK** ⚠ **and the BLOCK is `clean_tree` on this sitting's own uncommitted memo stamp — correct trip-gate behaviour** · spec footer trued **by running the harness** · **close-end sweep: 0 new inbound, MEASURED not inherited** · **outward acts: exactly the 2 declared file drops — no push, no forge call, no repo created, no visibility flip, no foreign wrapper copy edited.** ⏭ **Next: (a) ⭐ the 35 vaults need a range-scanning wrapper copy they can re-install FROM — a distribution problem that is OURS, named in the memo and now on the register; (b) A1 §3's 21-repo `license_state` enumeration onto the ledger — promised in ratified text, declined at this gate as its own pass; (c) Berthier's re-keying decision + Hestia's registry amendment (`ack_required: true`); (d) Pythia's cc, still owed; (e) ⭐ the P7a flip remains the critical path and every open precondition is another lane's; (f) the ADR-011 consolidation reading — seven layers; (g) shape B at the D4 revisit; > **⛩ 2026-08-24 (sixteenth sitting — SECOND on this local date) — ⭐⭐ **THE SEVEN LAND — 17 OF 18 DROPS, AND THE ONE REFUSAL WAS RIGHT** · ⭐ **A6 RATIFIED, AND IT RECORDS A DECISION ALREADY ACTED UPON** · ⛔ **F-P7b-m: OUR OWN HARNESS HAD ENCODED THE GAP IT EXISTED TO CATCH, AND PRINTED GREEN FOR TWO MONTHS** · ⛔ **F-P7b-n: THE PROBE SAID *GO* ON A DIRECTORY THAT HAS NEVER EXISTED** (`session_stanley_20260824_git_p7b_the_seven_land`).** Operator "please read the claude.md and let's continue the campaign" → open sweep (HEAD `98ee1bf`, clean, **0 untracked inbound**, ours 0 leases; peer leases **provisional at open by doctrine**, measured at each act) → 3-question gate (**deliver ALL SEVEN — outward window OPEN** · **RATIFY A6** · **ours-debt = the licensing pair + the probe header**) → plan approval (`please-read-the-claude-md-scalable-hejlsberg.md`). **(1) ⭐ ADR-011 A6 `accepted`** — §7.7 at the plan gate; **3 hunks, every one a single line** (frontmatter · A6 preamble · the Consequences annotation, done **BEFORE** the stamp so the signature landed on final text); A6 §1–§4 and `revision:` untouched. ⭐ **The stamp RECORDS A DECISION ALREADY ACTED UPON** — 2.1.0 and `census_secret_gate` already follow §4's positive predicate — **which is A4's own condition, named at the stamp rather than left for a later reader to infer.** **(2) ⭐ THE LICENSING HALF THAT WAS OURS IS NOW A CONTROL, NOT A LABEL.** `doctrine_gitops_block` **item 8** (`0.1.0→0.2.0`) · **ADR-013 Amendment A1 `proposed`** (D1–D7 **byte-identical**; the only two deletions in the whole diff are frontmatter) · **`_gitops_license_gate`** on `create-repo` + `set-visibility`. ⛩ **Scope judgment declared AT the gate, not after**: the operator authorized *a doctrine line + an amendment*, and I also built the check — because **ADR-011's Context opens with *"A label is not a control"***, and an amendment naming a check point while shipping no check is that sentence's next instance. **A1's two engineered clauses**: **§3 binds PROSPECTIVELY** (it does **not** make 21 already-placed repos errors at the ratification instant — ADR-014 A4 §1a's hazard, avoided by construction), and **§4 names `Git.aDNA` as a member of that set in the amendment itself.** ⭐ **The exposure asymmetry was the surprise**: the Codeberg set is *private and undistributed* so its ToS exposure is latent — **the public lane is actually distributing, and that is the one with a clock. An amendment aimed at the private lane first would have fixed the wrong lane.** **(3) ⛔⛔ F-P7b-m — THE HARNESS HAD ENCODED THE VERY GAP IT EXISTED TO CATCH.** Before A1, **six dispatch cases asserted AS PASSES** that an unlicensed Codeberg placement and an unlicensed public flip could be planned — green for two months. ⭐ ***A conformance suite written before a precondition existed asserts that precondition's ABSENCE, and goes on asserting it after the rule arrives.*** **Second limb, worse**: once the gate landed **in front of** the live guard, three `[safety]` cases greping the bare word `REFUSED` began matching **`REFUSED[license-gate]`** — **never reaching the guard they exist to test, still printing PASS.** ⭐ **A6's own fifth direction — a check that cannot DISCRIMINATE — reproduced by A6's ratifier, in the same sitting, by adding a second refusal path in front of the first.** *A check that greps for a WORD is hostage to every future caller of that word.* Both repaired: dispatch cases run in a licensed fixture, safety cases name the guard they mean. **Harness 42 → 62, both arms** — and **the green proved nothing until made red**: re-run against a **pre-gate** dispatch, **10 of the new cases fail**, ⚠ **and the survivors are the exit-code-only assertions** (a licensed placement plans fine either way) — *an exit code is a proxy; the output is the measurement*, the same lesson as 2.1.0's 4-pass/7-fail check, now earned on a second instrument. **(4) ⛔⛔ F-P7b-n — THE PROBE RETURNED `verdict: GO` AND THE COPY FAILED.** `WGS.aDNA/who/coordination/` **has never existed** (their surface is `who/comms/`, already holding three `coord_*` memos). The probe checked the **vault** and never the **directory** — and ⭐ **`dest_collision` reported `PASS — absent in target`, which was true and entirely misleading: the destination was absent because the whole DIRECTORY was absent.** **Absence read as health, on the one check whose job is to look at the destination** — the same family as `ln -sf` onto a missing target and A4 §4's `[ -d .git ]`. ⛩ **Only `--exec`'s exit code caught it: a probe whose verdict is corrected by the act it gates has the dependency backwards.** Fixed — `writedir_exists` BLOCKs, with a sabotage fixture (**a real vault with a missing write-dir**, so it cannot pass for the wrong reason) and a control; re-run against the exact send that returned GO, it now **REFUSEs**. ⚠ **And the self-indictment is exact**: that memo's frontmatter carried a flag to *confirm the addressee rather than assume*, and I did — read their `CLAUDE.md` at the object, confirmed Berthier — **then assumed the path.** ***Verifying the thing you thought to doubt is not the same as verifying the things you did not.*** **(5) ⭐ 17 OF 18 DROPS LANDED; THE ONE REFUSAL STANDS AND IS RECORDED WITH EVIDENCE.** `Inference.aDNA` (Pythia's cc on the ADR-014 memo) refused **twice** — **2 tracked edits mid-change** in her `who/coordination/` **plus** no `how/sessions/active/` at all ⇒ **UNKNOWN, and an unknown reading is never a silent pass.** Recorded **inside the memo Berthier now holds**, with the probe output quoted, so **the delay reads as our gap and he can route it himself.** ⚠ **Both aDNALabs and Home held LIVE LEASES at the act and both sends were GO** — because `declared_collision` reads declarations **positionally** and neither lease declared `who/coordination`. **Fixture C's discipline, paying in live operation.** **(6) ⛔ F-F23 VIOLATED BY MY OWN ORDERING, CAUGHT AND REPAIRED.** I copied **then** stamped ⇒ every peer briefly held a copy reading `status: staged` — *delivered mail asserting it was undelivered*. All 17 destinations refreshed, **each one first proven byte-identical to the committed pre-stamp source (`git show 667fa12:…`)** ⇒ provably the file we placed, untouched by anyone, and ours to refresh; anything else would have been **HANDS-OFF**. ⭐ *The guard is what makes a re-write of a peer's file legitimate rather than merely convenient.* **(7) ⚠ AND MY VERIFICATION SNIPPET REPRODUCED F-P7b-d** — `local B="$1" … src="who/coordination/$B"` — `local` declares every name before assigning, so the paths were empty and **every drop reported `DIFFERS`**. A **false red**, in the throwaway checker, on the exact bug `probe_peer_state.sh`'s own header documents. Fixed and re-run: **17 identical, 1 absent.** **Verification**: dry-run **62/62** · discrimination **10 red** against pre-gate · `probe --meta` **all fixtures reach failure, all 7 controls pass, `--exec` gates both ways** · deliveries **17 identical · all untracked peer-side · zero collateral (per-vault counts match the intended drop list exactly: aDNALabs 5 · aDNA 4 · Jupyter 4 · Home 3 · WGS 1 · Inference 0)** · A6 by hunk **3 × single-line** · ADR-013 **2 deletions, both frontmatter** · `preflight` **7 PASS / 1 BLOCK** ⚠ **and the BLOCK is `clean_tree` on this sitting's own 8 declared paths — correct trip-gate behaviour** · **close-end sweep: 0 untracked inbound — F-INTAKE-04 did NOT fire (second time in seven), recorded as MEASURED-AND-UNCHANGED because "no new inbound" is only a finding if someone looked** · **outward acts: exactly the 17 declared file drops — no push, no forge call, no repo created, no visibility flip.** ⏭ **Next: (a) ⭐ operator §7.7 on ADR-013 A1 — it is `proposed`, and until it is stamped the gate enforces a rule the ADR only proposes; (b) Pythia's cc, still owed — probe when her tree settles; (c) ⭐ the P7a flip remains the critical path and every open precondition is another lane's; (d) F-P7b-l's fix-then-retire ruling (Hestia + Berthier now hold the memo); (e) the ADR-011 consolidation reading — now SEVEN layers with A6; (f) the 21-repo license remediation set needs enumerating onto the ledger (A1 §3 promises it); (g) shape B at the D4 revisit; (h) obj 5 (3-precondition-blocked); (i) the fleet host-move wave; (j) the 9-row repoint runbook; (k) intake-log + CHANGELOG backfills.** **Debt register (7 close, 4 added):** ~~7 memos staged undelivered~~ **CLOSED — 17/18 drops; only Pythia's cc carries** · ~~A6 `proposed`~~ **CLOSED (ratified + delivered to Galileo in the same act)** · ~~`probe_peer_state.sh` header rationale~~ **CLOSED** · ~~`doctrine_gitops_block` licensing silence~~ **CLOSED (item 8)** · ~~ADR-013's ownerless predicate~~ **CLOSED (A1, `proposed`)** · **NEW: ADR-013 A1 needs operator §7.7** · **NEW F-P7b-m — a suite written before a precondition asserts its absence; R4 half still Rosetta's — both now asked with our half built**) · F-P7b-k (obj 5 gated on the flip's TLS) · `Videos.aDNA` has no remotes (Iris's) · LAVG origin (Cartographer's) · shape B / P4 far side · obj 5 · intake-log backfill (06-24→08-08 + all three 08-21 sittings) **+ CHANGELOG gap** · F-W6-a probe fix · fleet host-move wave · F-S158-01 limb 4 (Operations) · 9 wrapper repoints · 38 `FAIL_NONE` repos · 11 mesh rows · the 8 flip preconditions.

> **⛩ 2026-08-24 (fifteenth sitting) — ⭐⭐ **THE SHIPPED HOOK IS REPAIRED — AND MEASURING THE DEFECT FIRST SHOWED A6'S OWN FRAMING WAS WRONG IN BOTH DIRECTIONS** · ⭐ **F-C36 INVERTED: A CORRECT, PRE-AUTHORIZED RETIREMENT ON ANOTHER VAULT'S LANE WOULD HAVE SILENTLY UNGATED 44 REPOS** · ⛔ **TWO CONSUMERS FOUND MY DEFECT BEFORE I DID AND NEITHER REACHED ME** · ⚠ **MY OWN INDUCED POSITIVE FAILED TO BLOCK, ON THE CLAUSE I HAD JUST CITED** (`session_stanley_20260824_git_p7b_the_shim_that_holds_it_up`).** Operator "please read the claude.md and let's continue the campaign" → open sweep (HEAD `4ae46e9`, **1 untracked inbound**, ours 0 leases; peer leases **deliberately unprobed — nothing is sent**) → 3-question gate (**scope = intake + fix our own shipped defect, NON-OUTWARD** · **digest = add the new, KEEP the old as `PASS`** · **A6 HELD, not surfaced**) → plan approval (`please-read-the-claude-md-cached-map.md`). **(1) ⭐⭐ A6 SENT ME TO FIX A DEFECT AND ITS FRAMING WAS WRONG IN BOTH DIRECTIONS — only sweeping the class showed it.** The defect is real: `pre-push.gitleaks.sh:15` documented `ln -sf ../../git/hooks/… .git/hooks/pre-push`, a **pre-ADR-045** path; `ln -sf` succeeds against a missing target, git **silently skips** a hook it cannot execute, ⇒ **the repo reads *installed* and behaves *ungated*.** ⛔ **But A6 implies live exposure, and there was none**: measured **44 of 44** wrapper-carrying vaults still hold the root `git/` shim, and `census_secret_gate` — **a second, independent instrument** — reports **0 dangling installs fleet-wide.** **Nothing was ungated. The defect was LATENT.** ⭐ **And "latent" understates it in the other direction, which is the actual finding**: that shim is registered in `Home.aDNA` §C under the **ADR-045 wrapper-relocation batch** (175 unique root `<wrapper>` shims, `git` among them), window **`~2026-07-30` — LAPSED 25 days** — disposition **"batch-retire as one pre-authorized wave."** ⇒ **the hazard was never "some vaults are broken"; it was that one already-approved cleanup on Berthier's lane converts 44 working installs into 44 silently-ungated repos, with no error at any point.** ⛩ **F-C36 INVERTED**: Pandora paid for *"key a retirement condition to the observable it waits for, never to a phase expected to deliver it"* — **here a retirement keyed to the RIGHT observable fires CORRECTLY and breaks a third party's artifact**, because the artifact depended on the shim and never said so. ⭐ ***A dependency on a shim is a dependency — and neither end could see it***: I could not see the retire window, and the registry's ref-sweep condition cannot see **a relative path inside a shipped script**, which reads as a reference to nothing at all. **(2) ⛔⛔ TWO CONSUMERS FOUND MY DEFECT INDEPENDENTLY AND NEITHER REACHED THE ARTIFACT'S OWNER.** **Galileo** (`Jupyter.aDNA/how/federation/git/CLAUDE.md`) recorded it **exactly** — including the shim-retirement trigger, verified live from a fresh clone — and routed it *"as an observation; the shim's retire-condition is Hestia's, not this vault's."* **Correct about the shim. I ship the hook, and it never came to me**; it arrived days later, second-hand, inside a memo about something else. **WGS** went further and **had already implemented this exact repair** (`--git-common-dir`, `ln -sfn` to canonical, the A3 §2 wrapper-copy trap written out). ⭐ **The fix existed in the field while the vault that ships the artifact did not know there was a defect.** **Rule taken, against us: *a defect in a shipped artifact routes to the artifact's OWNER, not only to the party who can work around it. A finding routed to whoever can route AROUND it is not a finding reported.*** **(3) ⭐ FIXED AT CONTRACT 2.1.0** (`a1288f73…` → `04e6a745…`): install line **derives both ends** — `--git-common-dir` (never `--git-path`, which resolves symlinks; never `--absolute-git-dir`, which on a linked worktree returns a dir with **no `hooks/`**), source from the script's own location ⇒ **no shim, and no absolute path to this machine**. ⛩ **Adopted WGS's substance WITH CREDIT, changing one thing**: they hardcode `~/aDNA/Git.aDNA`, and *an absolute path to one machine inside the portability vault's own shipped artifact would be its own finding.* **`--self-test` now ASSERTS THE INSTALL RESOLVES** — four states where 2.0.0 printed green on all four: resolves (0) · **dangling/non-executable (1)** · absent (0, said loudly) · not-in-a-repo (skip, never a silent pass). ⛩ **The asymmetry is deliberate**: dangling is the **deceptive** state; absent is **honest** and already `FAIL_NONE` to the census — **A4 §5's own distinction**, which WGS restated, now enforced **in the hook** and not only in the census. **(4) ⚠⚠ MY FIRST INDUCED POSITIVE FAILED TO BLOCK — AND IT IS THE BEST EVIDENCE A5 §1 WAS WORTH RATIFYING.** I planted the **canonical AWS documentation example**; gitleaks **allowlisted** it, the hook printed `gitleaks clean ✓`, and **the plant PUSHED.** ⭐ **That is A5 §1's exact failure, reproduced BY A5's OWN AUTHOR, inside the drill validating the hook whose header I had just edited to cite A5 §1.** Second error in the same drill: I assembled the string at runtime believing that made it synthetic — **it protects the harness SOURCE, not the PLANT**; gitleaks scans committed content, contiguous either way. Re-run with a genuinely synthetic non-allowlisted plant: **BLOCKED**, ⭐ **and absent from the remote afterwards** — a third arm I added because *an exit code is a proxy and the remote's contents are the outcome.* **(5) ⭐ THE HARNESS GREW 31 → 42, AND THE GREEN PROVED NOTHING UNTIL I MADE IT RED.** 11 new install-surface cases, **both arms** (A4 §6 / A5 §2): 3 sabotage required to FAIL — including **a meta-control asserting the dangling fixture is *genuinely* dangling**, so the must-fail case cannot pass for the wrong reason — and 5 controls required to PASS. > **⛩ 2026-08-23 (thirteenth sitting — SECOND on this local date) — ⭐⭐ **OBJ 5 IS STAGED AND STAGING FOUND TWO REASONS NOT TO FIRE IT** · ⭐ **F-P7b-j: THE FOSS PREDICATE ADR-013 IS BUILT ON DOES NOT HOLD — 18 OF 19 CODEBERG REPOS, AND 3 OF 4 PUBLIC ONES INCLUDING THIS VAULT** · ⛔ **F-P7b-k: THE TRIP WOULD PUT A THIRD-PARTY PAT THROUGH PLAIN HTTP** · **A4 §4 corrected its own author before publication** (`session_stanley_20260823_git_p7b_the_foss_predicate`).** Operator "please read the claude.md and let's continue the campaign" → open sweep (HEAD `a2fc74d`, clean tree, **0 untracked inbound**, ours 0; `aDNA.aDNA` 0 · `Jupyter.aDNA` 0 · `Network.aDNA` 1 · `Home.aDNA` 1) → 3-question plan gate (**scope = STAGE obj 5 only, NO outward act** · **shape = Forgejo → Codeberg** · **surface all four held amendments as a packet**) → plan approval (`please-read-the-claude-md-replicated-abelson.md`). **(1) ⭐⭐ THE VERB WAS NEVER THE WORK.** `gitops_configure_mirror` is **already implemented** (`gitops_dispatch.sh:295` — Forgejo-native `push_mirrors`, `--idempotent`, with a **real** ADR-008 refusal path at `:168` for GitHub-origin) and harness-covered. ⇒ obj 5 is *"prove it federates and measure what that proves"*, not *"build the verb"* — a runbook written against the mission card's build-work reading would have re-implemented a working verb and proved nothing. **(2) ⛔ F-P7b-j — ADR-013's LOAD-BEARING DISCRIMINATOR IS UNSATISFIED.** ADR-013 permits the Codeberg lane *"**only** for FOSS-bound work"* and states 🚩 *"this ToS line is **exactly what separates row 2 from row 3**"*. The Codeberg set is private and undistributed; the exposure there is latent, and **the one with a clock running is the other one**. ⭐ **Root cause is a deliberate rule with a missing half**: `.adna/how/skills/skill_project_fork.md:100` — `rm -f LICENSE  # R4: no template LICENSE (project picks own license)` — and **nothing downstream ever asks the project to pick one** (not the fork skill, not `doctrine_gitops_block.md` which carries **no licensing token at all — ours**, not any wave runbook, not ADR-013 itself, **also ours**). ⇒ every vault is **born unlicensed by design** then placed on a host whose ToS assumes otherwise; the two licensed graphs (`Exchange` MIT · `Astro` BSL-1.1) are **exactly the two where a human decided** — the mechanism works whenever invoked and is never invoked. ⭐ **This is verbatim the rule Pandora paid for and we recorded at F-C36 five days ago — *key a condition to the observable it waits for, never to a phase expected to deliver it* — and we had its own instance in our tree the whole time.** **(3) ⛔⛔ F-P7b-k — THE BLOCKER THAT ONLY WRITING THE COMMANDS OUT COULD FIND.** Forgejo push-mirror auth rides **in the request body**, and ADR-004 **D5** *forces* **HTTPS+PAT** for LFS repos ⇒ §4.4 sends **`CODEBERG_TOKEN` to `http://10.43.0.28:3300`** — **plain HTTP** — and the forge then **stores it at rest**. ⭐ **This is F-C36's coherence item acquiring the limb that makes it bite**: Pandora's finding held that `insecure = true` against **this exact endpoint** is *"consistent today — declared, group-gated, plain HTTP, **zero pulls ever**"*, and **"zero pulls ever" is the clause that made it tolerable**. Obj 5 would be **the first act to send a live third-party secret through it**, and it would stop being true *silently*. It also collides head-on with the flip runbook's **P2 — *"no per-client insecure-skip flags, ever"***. ⇒ **OBJ 5 IS TRANSITIVELY GATED ON THE P7a FLIP's TLS PRECONDITIONS — a sequencing dependency that existed nowhere in writing before this runbook.** ⚠ *The mesh is not the answer: Nebula encrypts the transport, and that is real, but it does not make it correct to hand a **third-party** credential to a service over a plaintext application-layer channel and let it store what cannot then be withdrawn.* **(4) ⭐ A4 §4 CORRECTED ITS OWN AUTHOR, BEFORE PUBLICATION.** Planning measured a **hand-picked 7** assembled *from this STATE file's narrative* and got **"0 of 7 licensed"**; re-measured against the **actual population** (every vault whose `origin` resolves to `codeberg.org`, deduped — 22 dirs → **19 repos**, three sharing with shims) it is **18 of 19**. · two inbound discharged, both of them defects in our own documents (`session_stanley_20260823_git_p7b_the_second_end`).** Operator "please read the claude.md and let's continue the campaign" → open sweep (HEAD `9c0f958`, clean tree, **2 untracked inbound**, `Jupyter.aDNA` **CLEAR**, `aDNA.aDNA` **ACTIVE**) → 3-question plan gate (**scope = deliveries + F-P7b-i + both intakes, obj 5 NOT opened** · **Rosetta delivery authorized on the narrow premise that its lease does not declare `who/coordination/`** · **ADR-015's phantom citation corrected IN PLACE and VISIBLY, no rev bump**) → plan approval (`please-read-the-claude-md-atomic-naur.md`). **(1) ⭐⭐ F-P7b-i CLOSED — and the fix is a tool, not a resolution.** [[how/tests/probe_peer_state|probe_peer_state.sh]] (read-only, 6 checks, each stating its `fails_when:` at the point of definition) takes **`--exec`**, which runs the command **only on a GO** ⇒ *"probe in the same command as the act"* stops being a discipline someone has to remember and becomes **a property of the instrument**. ⛔ **Its `--meta` FAILED on its first run and the failure was the finding, not the bug**: one root cause (`local t="$1" dir="$t/…"` — `local` declares every name before assigning, so `$t` is unset when `dir` expands and `set -u` kills the shell mid-check) made **three sabotage fixtures report `ok` while the check was CRASHING** — they wanted NOTPASS, and a dead shell reads as NOTPASS. **Only the known-good controls exposed it.** ⭐ *That is **F-P7b-d's exact shape, recurring inside the instrument written to prevent it**, and it is ADR-011 A4 §6's "**and** a control required to PASS" earning its keep for the second time in four days.* **(2) ⭐ THE INSTRUMENT'S FIRST LIVE READING DISAGREED WITH THE OPEN SWEEP — AND WAS RIGHT.** `aDNA.aDNA` read **0 live** where the open sweep had read **1 ACTIVE**; verified independently — Rosetta's lease had moved to `history/` `status: completed` **mid-sitting**. ⚠ **So the operator's conditional authorization was MOOT rather than exercised**: its premise dissolved, and the strict reading (*any lease blocks*) and the permissive one now **agree**. Recorded as such, because a conditional authorization that never had to be relied upon must not be logged as though it was. ⭐ **And the direction matters**: F-P7b-i fired **benign** this time — a stale reading that happens to be permissive is still stale; the only thing that changed is whether anyone would have noticed. **(3) ⛔ THE PREDICATE RULE, GUARDED BY CONSTRUCTION THIS TIME.** `declared_collision` does **not** grep the lease for our directory name — it reads declarations from a `declared_files:` block or a `Files declared` row **and nowhere else**. ⭐ **Fixture C is not hypothetical**: `aDNA.aDNA`'s live lease contains the literal string `who/coordination/` inside a documented sweep command while declaring an entirely different file set — **a grep-based check REFUSES that send and is wrong.** The same error cost us twice this month (`lfs_rehydration` false-BLOCKING on the two files that merely *quote* the LFS signature; a peer's census adjudicating a hook on a string in the hook's **own comment block**, moving 68 repos). **(4) ⭐ BOTH DELIVERIES LANDED, EACH THROUGH THE PROBE VIA `--exec`.** → **Galileo** (`Jupyter.aDNA`, **THIRD attempt** — refused 08-21 by operator scope, refused 08-22 by the probe): `cmp` identical, 10630 B, untracked, **exactly one file in their tree**. → **Rosetta** (`aDNA.aDNA`, second attempt): identical, 8887 B, untracked, ours the only file we added. `delivered_commit: a889525` stamped **before** each copy (F-F23) ⇒ zero-delta. ⇒ **two carried debts CLOSE.** ⚠ **And the act found a defect in its own guard**: `printf '--exec exit…'` — a format string starting with `--` is parsed as **options** by bash's printf and dies. The `cp` had already succeeded so the error was cosmetic, **but it printed after the act and reads as "the delivery errored"** — *a guard whose report is unreadable at the moment it matters has a broken last mile.* Fixed, `--meta` re-run green. **(5) ⭐ F-F37 CLOSED — the citation that resolved to nothing.** Ilmarinen routed rather than fixed-only-theirs: ADR-015 §Constraint inputs cited `Network.aDNA/who/governance/ruling_2026_08_07_scoped_s8_rd_forge.md`, **which has never existed** (re-verified **at the object** here — `who/governance/` holds three `ruling_*` files, none of them this). Repointed at both sites to the two sources that **do** resolve, **recorded rather than silently overwritten**: *an auditor who follows a dead path cannot tell a broken pointer from a fabricated authority.* ⛔ **Ratified-document discipline held**: diff verified **by hunk** = exactly **2 hunks** (frontmatter + preamble); `revision:` still **4**; D1–D5 and Amendment A1 **byte-identical**. ⚖ **Their transferable half, and it is the better finding**: *"a reference that is always restated is a reference that is never resolved"* — the ruling's content was quoted in full in both documents, so for fourteen days nobody ever needed to follow the pointer. **(6) ⭐ F-C36 — THE FLIP HAD NO NOTICE LIST AT ALL.** Pandora (`Container.aDNA`) enrolled themselves; runbook gains **§1e**, a **fourth §6a retirement row**, and a forward pointer **on the P2 row itself**. ⛔ **The coherence item is the part that matters**: P2's criterion names *"no per-client insecure-skip flags, **ever**"* while a **finalized** consumer package prescribes `insecure = true` against **that exact endpoint** — consistent today (declared, group-gated, plain HTTP, zero pulls ever), **contradictory the moment P2 is claimed complete**, with neither side's records pointing at the other. ⭐ *That is the shape that reads green and is not.* ⚠ **Their honest half, volunteered**: *"a dispatch list cannot see a parameter bound in someone else's file — only the holder can enrol itself."* ⭐ **And the rule they paid for, aimed at a class we also have: *key a retirement condition to the observable it waits for, never to a phase expected to deliver it — a phase can complete by deciding.*** Theirs read *"until Git P7a"*; **P7a completed by deciding and the endpoint is still plain HTTP**, so read literally it would have retired the *declaration* and left the *plaintext*. **(7) Doctrine landed**: the both-ends sweep is now binding in [[how/sessions/AGENTS|how/sessions/AGENTS.md]] for **inbound and leases alike**, with the open-end reading written as **provisional** and the predicate stated (*a lease that merely exists is not a refusal*). **Verification**: ADR-015 by hunk ✅ · preflight ✅ **8 PASS / 0 BLOCK** at a clean tree (no regression) · `--meta` ✅ all fixtures reach failure, controls pass, `--exec` gates **both** ways · deliveries ✅ `cmp` 2/2, both non-empty, both untracked, zero collateral files · close-end sweep ✅ **0 new inbound, 0 leases at any target**. ⏭ **Next: (a) obj 5 mirror-mesh federation — needs a SECOND forge and the only candidate is hosted Codeberg, so it is outward with a FOSS-ToS question: name it AT the gate, not inside the work; (b) operator §7.7 on ADR-011 A3+A4+A5 and ADR-014 A4 (held, not lapsed); (c) shape B at the D4 revisit — obj 4 closed on shape A and P4 is UNBUILT, so "carries subnet context" is still unlicensed; (d) the fleet host-move wave (D5-unblocked); (e) the 9-row repoint runbook; (f) the tooling cascade; (g) the intake-log backfill — its own pass.** **Debt register (4 close, 0 added):** ~~Galileo reply~~ **CLOSED (3rd attempt)** · ~~A5 → Rosetta~~ **CLOSED** · ~~F-P7b-i~~ **CLOSED (instrument + doctrine)** · ~~F-F37 phantom citation~~ **CLOSED** · **F-C36 closed on our side** (Pandora executes at the window) · **`freshness_mode` conf patch — still blocks ALL 11 mesh rows** · LAVG origin (**Cartographer's**, sequenced behind Berthier) · shape B / P4 far side · obj 5 · intake-log backfill (06-24→08-08 **+ all three 08-21 sittings**) · tooling cascade (incl. spec's stale `23/23` footer vs harness 31) · F-W6-a probe fix · fleet host-move wave · F-S158-01 limb 4 (Operations) · 9 wrapper repoints · 32 `FAIL_NONE` repos · 11 UNREACHABLE mesh rows = unmeasured, not healthy · the 8 flip preconditions.

> **⛩ 2026-08-22 (eleventh sitting) — ⭐⭐ **D3 §2 IS RATIFIED — AND THE RATIFICATION ARRIVED MID-SESSION, CATCHING TWO DEFECTS NINETY SECONDS FROM COMMIT** · ⛔ **BOTH AUTHORIZED DELIVERIES REFUSED BY THE PROBE — AND BOTH REFUSALS WERE RIGHT** · **the class we swept for had two members and a peer found the second** (`session_stanley_20260822_git_p7b_the_ruling_lands`).** Operator "please read the claude.md and let's continue the campaign" → open sweep (HEAD `11d1cd5`, clean tree, **1 untracked inbound**, our leases 0; `aDNA.aDNA`/`Jupyter.aDNA`/`Network.aDNA` **read clear**) → 3-question plan gate (**scope = the ruling + the two owed memos, obj 5 NOT opened** · ⭐ **the ruling enters as an AMENDMENT — ratified text not edited, no rev 5** · **both deliveries authorized, re-probed at the act**) → plan approval (`please-read-the-claude-md-tranquil-thunder.md`). **(1) ⭐⭐ [[what/decisions/adr_015_lighthouse_integration_architecture|ADR-015 Amendment A1]] §1 `accepted` — D3 §2 TAKES SHAPE (a), PROSPECTIVE-ONLY.** Venus ruled it (S398) and **the operator signed it at HER S399 gate**, scope bounded in writing to *"D3 §2 and nothing else"*. ⛔ **F-INTAKE-04 fired a FIFTH consecutive time and was load-bearing again**: the pre-commit re-check read **2** inbound where the open sweep read **1**, and the second memo *carried the signature on the very clause being authored*. It caught two things: **(i) her RATIFIED text differs from her DRAFTED text by one clause** (`; ratified stanley 2026-08-22`) ⇒ our A1 had already been certified **"verbatim 19/19, zero drift" against a SUPERSEDED draft** — ⭐ **a verbatim check names the object it compared against, or it certifies the wrong thing correctly** (the predicate rule's *fourth* direction in 72 hours: not what is measured but **against which object** — and it printed a green); re-verified **20/20**. **(ii) A1 could not ship `proposed`** on a clause already signed — **F-P7a-l's shape**, ninety seconds from being committed. **(2) ⛩ THE SCOPE QUESTION WAS PUT, NOT ASSUMED.** The signature was taken against **Venus's clause**; our A1 also carries **§2** (why (a) not (b)) and **§3** (a finding against ourselves), **authored after the signature and never read by the signer**. Ruled: **§1 `accepted` · §2–§3 NON-NORMATIVE, not ratified, not citable as such.** ⭐ **This is Venus's own discipline applied to her ruling** — *"I put the scope question to the operator explicitly rather than reading a broad yes into a narrow one"* — and stamping the wrapper would have been **exactly the broad-yes move she had declined one memo earlier on the same clause**. The gate field also **names that the signature is held on a PEER'S RECEIPT and was not witnessed here** — the strongest form available (primary account, co-signing peer, delivered as the instrument, scope bounded in writing) and **not** the transcribed-status class, *but a gate record is the one place that distinction must be visible rather than inferred*. **Diff verified by hunk: 4 deletions, ALL frontmatter; D3 §2's ratified sentence untouched; `revision:` still 4.** **(3) ⭐ HER REASONING BEAT OUR FINDING.** We proved the subject set empty **empirically**; she showed it empty **definitionally, one paragraph above the clause** — D3 **§1** already assigns git-homed graphs to the git lane, so **§2 contradicts §1 of the same D**. ⛔ **Shape (b) ruled *dangerous*, not merely inferior**: its honest subject is the **ADR-004 ceremony + ADR-020 consent slice** — *the thing §1 declares must NOT retire* ⇒ (b) would have **manufactured the consent regression our own §2 warned of**. ⚠ **A1 §3, against ourselves: SECOND TIME IN FOUR DAYS an amendment of ours repairs a clause its own neighbouring paragraph contradicted** (cf. ADR-011 A5 §2 — A2 §3 vs §4, eleven lines apart; ADR-014 A4 on A3 §1) ⇒ ⭐ **the pattern is the finding: our review reads clauses one at a time, and every one of these defects is visible only when two adjacent clauses are read against each other.** **(4) ⭐ F-P7b-b CORRECTED — TWO MEMBERS, NOT ONE, AND NOT THE SAME CASE.** We flagged `LAVentureGraph.aDNA` and wrote *"the single exception"* **without sweeping for the class**; **Venus swept every `*.aDNA` and found `operations_jake.aDNA`**. ⛩ The rule is **not** *"no forge origins"* — under **ADR-006** it is *"`origin` = canonical home"*, so Jake's vault under **ADR-019** is **not touched on a naming convention**, while LAVG (GitHub demoted to `rollback`) **is** misplaced; placement ruled (`origin`→canonical, forge as `mesh-rd`). ⛔ **Execution is Cartographer's**, sequenced beside Berthier's same-day memo on the same repo. ⚠ *"A finding closed at its instance is not a finding closed"* — **our own sentence** (ADR-011 A4 §3), **third instance in three days**, and **this time a peer ran our sweep for us; the sweep is one command.** **(5) ⛔ BOTH DELIVERIES REFUSED AT THE ACT (`03:30:46Z`) — AND BOTH REFUSALS WERE RIGHT.** Authorized at the gate on an open sweep reading **0 active for both**; the probe, re-read **in the same command as the `cp`**, found both peers had taken leases in the interval — **and both leases are working in our target directory**: **Galileo** holds `gentle_lexicon` `open`, class line *"one outbound"* — **writing coordination mail**; **Rosetta** holds `haussmann_p3_3_o3_reduced` `active`, 25 dirty, leg **A1 = "Venus intake"**, **two untracked inbound already in `who/coordination/`**. Refusals recorded **in the memos themselves with evidence** (named lease · its status · its own declared work) so the delay reads as *our* gap, not their unavailability. **(6) ⚠ THE DEBT REGISTER WAS UNDERSTATING ITS OWN RANGE** — it names the intake-log backfill as *"06-24→08-08"*; measured, the log has **no rows for any of the three 2026-08-21 sittings**, every one of which took inbound. **Corrected, not silently backfilled** (filling them while writing this row would make the gap unfindable). ⏭ **Next: (a) ⭐ RE-ATTEMPT BOTH DELIVERIES when the leases clear — probe first, both are severity-carrying (Galileo's §4(b) reading concerns 68 repos; Rosetta's gate can fire with no date); (b) ⭐ EXTEND THE BOTH-ENDS SWEEP TO LEASES (F-P7b-i) — cheap, and it is now the only unguarded end; (c) obj 5 mirror-mesh (own gate; needs a second forge — the only candidate is hosted Codeberg, so it is outward with a FOSS-ToS question attached); (d) operator §7.7 on ADR-011 A3+A4+A5 and ADR-014 A4; (e) shape B at the D4 revisit; (f) the fleet host-move wave (D5-unblocked); (g) the 9-row repoint runbook; (h) the tooling cascade.** **Debt register (0 close, 1 added, 1 corrected):** **Galileo reply — SECOND held attempt** · **A5 → Rosetta — refused at the act** · **NEW F-P7b-i: lease sweep needs the both-ends discipline** · **LAVG origin → now CARTOGRAPHER'S**, sequenced behind Berthier · **`freshness_mode` conf patch — still blocks ALL 11 mesh rows** · shape B / P4 far side · obj 5 · **intake-log backfill CORRECTED to 06-24→08-08 *plus* all three 08-21 sittings** · tooling cascade (incl. spec's stale `23/23` footer vs harness 31) · F-W6-a probe fix · fleet host-move wave · F-S158-01 limb 4 (Operations) · 9 wrapper repoints · 32 `FAIL_NONE` repos · 11 UNREACHABLE mesh rows = unmeasured, not healthy · the 8 flip preconditions.

The −5/+5 are **one set** — `what/lab` + 4 worktrees, closed by **their owner's regular-file v2 install**, not by our runbook. Had §4(b) been right those 68 rows would be ungated and coverage would read **7/118 (6%)**. **The arithmetic closes on the digest reading and not on the grep one.** Census `.md` gains a **freshness banner, body NOT rewritten** (a census is a dated reading). **(3) ADR-011 A5 `proposed`** (A2 §4 is **ratified** and **NOT edited**): §1 **the plant must be SYNTHETIC and NON-ALLOWLISTED** — Galileo's first induced positive used the **canonical AWS documentation example pair**; a `cp` to its answer **overwrites the tracked wrapper P7a rows 8–9 reserve** (happened in their tree, caught by md5, reverted); `--absolute-git-dir` on a worktree returns a dir with **no `hooks/` at all** — **A4 §4's exclusion by a second independent predicate**; repairs target **`--git-common-dir`** · §4 **a predicate matching a hook's own documentation is not a measurement**. **Consequences ⚠**: *every* roster induced positive records **that** a plant blocked and **none records what was planted** — checked at source, **including our own row**; none voided, none re-adjudicable. **(4) ⭐⭐ OBJ 4 CLOSES — the round-trip PASSED.** Preflight fresh **8 PASS / 0 BLOCK**; all three §D1.5b controls **re-fired** (`303`+`Location` @ `num_redirects=0` · `401` · `200`);
