> **⛩ 2026-08-21 (ninth sitting) — "THE LANE WAS ALREADY RUNNING": P7b obj 4's reconciliation half DELIVERED · ⭐ **D3 §2 RETIRES AN EMPTY SET** · the round-trip STAGED not run · **four findings, two of them against our own instruments** (`session_stanley_20260821_git_p7b_context_sync_preflight`).** Operator "please read the claude.md and let's continue the campaign" → open sweep (HEAD `c5dddfe`, 0 leases, **1 untracked inbound**) → 3-question plan gate (**scope = P7b obj 4** · Hestia = **hold + record the second refusal** · ⛔ **NON-OUTWARD ONLY**) → plan approval (`please-read-the-claude-md-jaunty-fiddle.md`). **The constraint was named at the gate rather than worked around: obj 4's exit criterion is a push/clone round-trip, so under a non-outward ruling THE OBJECTIVE CANNOT CLOSE.** What closed is everything that must be true before the wire is touched. **(1) ⚠ A CORRECTION AGAINST OURSELVES AT THE OPEN SWEEP, BEFORE IT REACHED AN ARTIFACT.** The first reachability probe hit **port 3000** and read the R&D forge as **dead**. 3000 is **container-internal** behind the `:3300` publish — ADR-015 D1 §109 says so in terms. Re-probed at 3300: **LIVE**, Forgejo `15.0.6+gitea-1.22.0`. Had that reading been written down this session would have opened by reporting a live instance as down. **The probe was wrong, not the forge.** **(2) ⭐ BOTH §D1.5b INSTRUMENT CONTROLS FIRED LIVE** (`2026-08-21T23:37Z`): `/raw/main/README.md` → **`303` + `Location`** with **`num_redirects=0`** — the struck "redirect chain empty" column reading *empty* on a live redirect, **F-F25's mechanism observed rather than argued** — and `/api/v1/user` → **`401`**. Recorded in [[how/campaigns/campaign_git_genesis/missions/p7a_flip_staging/flip_runbook|flip_runbook §2]] as a **Hopper-side observation, ⛔ EXPLICITLY NOT A DISCHARGE of Ilmarinen's P4 row** (which needs the whole §2 table captured in one pass): *firing a control is evidence the instrument works, not evidence the capture was taken.* **(3) ⛔ F-P7b-a — D3 §2's CONVERGENCE CRITERION RETIRES AN EMPTY SET, on two independent axes.** It retires *"scheduled tarball transmission **of git-homed vaults**"*. Measured at Venus's objects: **all 9 live payloads are NODE vaults** (`node_adna_*`) and **ADR-004 §a.1 excludes `.git/` by contract** ⇒ no git-homed graph rides the ceremony lane; and transmissions are **event-driven, not scheduled** (no cadence across `06-11…08-08`; the only nightly schedule is a **reconciliation pass over placed mirrors**, a different operation). ⇒ **at D4 the clause fires and nothing retires.** Sibling of **F-P7a-f** one level up: *a retirement criterion with no subject retires nothing.* ⚠ **And the adjacent set is NOT empty — that is the real hazard**: **ADR-020**'s consented `campaign_state/` slice (4 fields · titles opt-in · **NEVER-transits list** · one-line revocation) rides *inside* the node tarball, so a loose reading at D4 — *"replicas carry the context now, retire the tarballs"* — would **replace a four-field consented summary with whole repositories**: a **consent regression wearing the costume of a convergence**. **We wrote that clause and she co-signed it — it is ours before it is hers.** Memo **STAGED** (`ack_required`); the amendment is her clause. **(4) ⛔ F-P7b-b — THE LANE WAS ALREADY RUNNING; obj 4 and D3 both read as greenfield.** Measured: **20 distinct vaults** carry a mesh remote across **THREE** fabrics (`mesh-rd` 11 answering · `luke-mesh` 5 · `mesh` 6, both unreachable from here) — **`Git.aDNA` among them.** Obj 4's real content is *prove the round-trip*, not *build the lane*; a runbook written against the greenfield reading would have staged the wrong work. ⚠ **`LAVentureGraph.aDNA` carries the R&D forge as its `origin`** (GitHub demoted to `rollback`) — breaks ADR-014 D2 naming, and worse, **a graph's primary home is a spike instance**. **Flagged, not edited.** **(5) ⛔ F-P7b-c — THE RATIFICATION IS NOT ON THE LANE THAT D3 DECLARES TO BE THE FABRIC.** Our replica tip is `169eff4`; local `c5dddfe`. Read **at the object**: `git cat-file -p 169eff4:…adr_015….md` → **`status: proposed`**. **A peer reading `Git.aDNA` over the git lane today sees the P7a gate as OPEN.** This morning we filed **F-P7a-l** for a gate document understating its own gate *in its text*; this is the same document understating the same gate *in its distribution* — **twice in one day, same field.** ⛔ **Deliberately NOT fixed by pushing**: whether the lag is a *defect* needs a declared `freshness_mode`, and A4 §1a's `pending_declaration` **exists in no conf** (Berthier's pen) ⇒ a push makes our row green and leaves the other ten **unadjudicable**. **F-A4-01's cost, measured rather than argued.** **(6) ⭐ F-P7b-d — THE META-CONTROL COULD NOT FAIL, AND ONLY THE KNOWN-GOOD CONTROL CAUGHT IT.** New read-only instrument [[how/tests/preflight_context_sync|preflight_context_sync.sh]] (8 checks, each stating its **failure condition at the point of definition**; secret-gate **delegated to the census**, never re-implemented — two adjudications is how they drift). Its first `--meta` run showed **7/7 sabotage fixtures `ok`** — and all six known-good rows **FAIL**. Cause: `meta_expect` adjudicated on the `pass` counter, which `$( … )` discards in a subshell ⇒ **always 0 ⇒ always NOTPASS** ⇒ **every sabotage row was `ok` by construction, testing nothing.** *A meta-control that cannot fail, committed inside the harness that forbids exactly that (**F-P7a-f**).* **A4 §6's "and a control required to PASS" paid for itself the first time it ran.** Fixed to read the check's own emitted row; **7 fixtures reach failure + 6 controls pass.** Two further instrument defects found and fixed **while producing the table they feed**: the lanes sweep took the **first** matching remote (so `Forgejo.aDNA` read `DIVERGED` by-name and `UNREACHABLE` in-sweep — *same tree, minutes apart, both correct*; **an unlabelled row is not a measurement** ⇒ one row per remote, named), and **`size-pack` alone under-reported toward "cheap"** (`LAVentureGraph` read **`0 bytes`** while holding **26 MiB of loose objects**). ⛔ **And a third, caught by running the instrument against its own vault AFTER the commit: `lfs_rehydration` FALSE-BLOCKED on two files — *this script and the runbook documenting it*, which merely quote the pointer signature.** A pointer is the signature **on line 1**, not the string anywhere in the file. **A false BLOCK stops a legitimate round-trip**, and it is the *same root error* as a check that cannot fail — **the check did not state precisely what it measures.** Line-anchored + first-line-confirmed; fixture E still catches a real pointer. **F-P7b-e.** **(7) Round-trip runbook STAGED** → [[how/campaigns/campaign_git_genesis/missions/p7b_staging/context_sync_runbook|context_sync_runbook]] — **fires nothing**, per-section gates. ⛔ **P4 is not merely ungated but UNBUILT**: there is **no reachable far side**, and a same-node clone *"proves the forge stored the bytes"* — **a weaker claim than the exit gate**, so the two shapes are tabled with the sentence each licenses and **the spike report must say which one ran**. §4.5 pins `diff -r --no-dereference` **as load-bearing**: without it `diff` follows the `git →how/federation/git` symlink and **a broken link compares equal** — a check that could not fail, inside a runbook about checks that cannot fail. **(8) Hestia E3 — SECOND REFUSAL, recorded with evidence not assertion** (§0a): her session `active`, tree dirty, and **`who/coordination/` is named in its `declared_files`** — *the lease names our target directory*, which is what makes the refusal correct rather than merely cautious. Honest counter-reading stated in-file (her log says the agent legs are closed) **and rejected**: *a lease is released by its holder, not by a reader's inference that the holder looks finished.* Three delivery fields stay `null`. **(9) Venus's inbound intaken by name**; her §1 was **stale at arrival** (the line-12 edit went in at `8892faa`) — *the class she had just finished fixing* — **noted, not filed**. ⏭ **Next: (a) ⭐ open an outward window → fire the round-trip (shape A) → obj 4 closes; (b) deliver the staged Venus memo (D3 §2 amendment) + the staged Hestia memo when leases clear; (c) obj 5 mirror-mesh; (d) operator §7.7 on ADR-011 A3/A4 + ADR-014 A4; (e) the fleet host-move wave (D5-unblocked); (f) the 10-row repoint runbook; (g) the tooling cascade.** **Debt register (carried + additions):** **Hestia E3 (staged, 2nd refusal)** · **Venus D3-§2 memo (staged)** · **`freshness_mode` conf patch — blocks adjudication of ALL 11 mesh rows** · **`LAVentureGraph` origin-on-spike** · **11 UNREACHABLE mesh rows = unmeasured, not healthy** · tooling cascade (incl. spec's stale `23/23` footer vs harness 31) · intake-log backfill 06-24→08-08 · F-W6-a probe fix · fleet host-move wave · F-S158-01 limb 4 (Operations) · the 10 wrapper repoints · 31 `FAIL_NONE` repos · `.adna/` template gate (Rosetta's) · the 8 flip preconditions.

> **⛩ 2026-08-21 (eighth sitting) — "P7a CLOSES": ⭐ **[[what/decisions/adr_015_lighthouse_integration_architecture|ADR-015]] RATIFIED `accepted` AT REV 4 — BOTH GATE LIMBS SHUT — P7a IS COMPLETE** · the two OWED items discharged · a stale claim found **inside our own gate document** (`session_stanley_20260821_git_p7a_closes`).** Operator "please read the claude.md and let's continue the campaign" → open sweep (clean tree `169eff4`, no active sessions, **no untracked inbound**) → 2-question plan gate (**§7.7 = ADR-015 rev 4 ONLY**, ADR-011 A3/A4 + ADR-014 A4 held `proposed` by operator choice · **scope = the owed debt**) → plan approval (`please-read-the-claude-md-parsed-octopus.md`). **(1) ⛔ THE OPEN SWEEP FOUND THE GATE DOCUMENT UNDERSTATING ITS OWN GATE.** ADR-015's Ratification block still read *"held `staged` … **never delivered** … the gate is **not** half-closed on it."* **True when written** (08-20, sixth sitting) and **stale within the same day** — her memo moved that evening. That block is **the artifact an operator reads when deciding whether to take §7.7**, so left as it was it described a blocker that no longer existed and its honest reading was *"do not ratify yet."* This is the **transcribed-status class we filed against peers in three consecutive sittings** (F-P7a-b · F-P7a-d · F-P7a-g) and flagged in Venus's own frontmatter on 08-20 — **occurring inside our own gate record, on the one field where being wrong costs a phase.** Corrected **before** stamping, never quietly; filed **F-P7a-l**. *(Venus has since fixed her `status:` field — our §2 correction was taken; ours had gone unexamined a day longer.)* **(2) ⭐ RATIFIED — and ratification moved NOTHING.** `status: accepted`, **rev 4**, date **2026-08-21**; **no rev 5 — ratification is not a revision.** Limb 1 = Venus's concurrence, **delivered** S392, `concurs_with @ revision 4 (EXTENDED, read at the object 847bccb)`, both moved clauses **verified by her independently before extending**. Limb 2 = operator §7.7. **Proof the object did not move: `git diff 847bccb` touches exactly TWO hunks — frontmatter (2–29) and the Ratification block (216+); the Decisions section begins at line 34 and is untouched.** Stated in-file because moving the object *at the ratification instant*, holding a concurrence that names its revision, would have been F-P7a-b's **worst** instance rather than its last. **⇒ All four ADR-012 seams decided (D1 addressing/TLS · D2 identity bridge · D3 context-sync coexist-now-converge-**by lane** · D4 production placement) + D5 — which **UNBLOCKS THE FLEET HOST-MOVE WAVE**.** **(3) E2 FOLDED → the flip runbook's new [[how/campaigns/campaign_git_genesis/missions/p7a_flip_staging/flip_runbook|§1d]]** (**not** into D1.3 — Venus offered both homes with no preference; the runbook is where a scheduler reads, and folding into D1.3 would have moved the object at the stamp. ⛔ *Do not promote it later.*) **Every fact re-verified in `Network.aDNA` at source rather than transcribed — and that caught a drift.** ⛔ **HER NUMBER WAS TWELVE; THE COUNT IS THIRTEEN.** Regency P1 NO-GO was accurate at her writing and her own `STATE.md:46` now logs a **13th** (helper-present) — **F-DECL-03 exactly: a measurement recorded without its timestamp is a claim with a hidden expiry.** No fault attaches to her; **the finding is about transcription**, and it is Pythia's doctrine, adopted two days prior, paying for itself. Also folded, and **absent from her memo to us**: ⭐ **FileVault being ON makes the LOSS case WORSE** (it is the key's only at-rest protection — theft posture improves, but a failure costing the FileVault key renders the CA unrecoverable **even holding the physical disk**) · the **second authority** (PercySleep holds CA write authority to Waypoint M5 — P0 measured *the file, not the authority graph*) · and one thing **explicitly ruled NOT to transfer** (F-S353-01's TTY requirement is `nebula-cert`-specific; an X.509 CA inherits none of it — said so nobody imports it wrongly). **(4) E3 — the memo to Hestia is WRITTEN; ⛔ the send was REFUSED BY ITS OWN PROBE, and the debt therefore does NOT close** ([[who/coordination/coord_2026_08_21_hopper_to_hestia_adr015_d13_trust_anchor_assignment|memo]], `ack_required`, **`status: staged`**). `Home.aDNA` was quiet at open (12:40); at the copy, `session_stanley_20260821_p5_4_gate_ruling_and_intake` was **`active`** (12:41) with a dirty tree ⇒ **single-writer lease holds, do not write.** *Venus refused a send into this same inbox on 08-20 for the same reason — **the GO authorises the send; the probe governs the moment.*** ⚠ **And a defect of ours inside the fix**: the memo was first written with **`status: outbound_delivered` and `delivered_at` already filled in, before any copy** — **F-DEL-01, pre-stamping a delivery ahead of the act**, committed by the vault that has filed that exact class twice this week. The probe caught it; **the probe is not what should have.** Corrected to `null` + disclosed in the memo's own §0. **E3 stays OWED until the lease clears.** **D1.3 assigns trust-anchor distribution to her channel and she has never read the document — through four revisions and a ratification.** The memo puts **the disclosure before the ask** (she is told it is `accepted` before she is asked anything; the reverse ordering would let it read as a proposal she could have shaped), **credits Venus by name and quotation**, and records the pattern against ourselves: **the fourth capability this single ADR assigns to a vault with no part in the sentence** — three Venus caught at rev 3 (mesh-internal resolver · fabric-id registry · `forge` service class, **none of which existed**), **and this is the one that got through ratification uncaught**. Ask = **one ruling**, with the honest timing named: *not urgent* (fallback-only, behind a ×13 gate), *real*. Deliberately raises **no** other Home-side item — a queue of unrelated asks inside a memo about not assigning her things she did not ask for would be the same error one level up. **(5) P7a mission card `completed` + AAR** (Worked: staging the runbook **before** ratification meant the operator ratified D1 knowing its execution cost; Didn't: four capabilities assigned in prose; Finding: **F-P7a-f/-l — a measurement that cannot fail is not a control, and a status field is a claim with a hidden expiry**; Change: **prose does not assign capability**). Charter P7a row ✅; **P7 stays 🔵 active** (P7b obj 4–5 outstanding; the `Lighthouse.aDNA` go/no-go is still P7b's). **Findings: F-P7a-l** (*a gate record is the least affordable place for a self-declared status field*) · **F-P7a-g CLOSED** · **F-DECL-03 re-instanced live**. ⏭ **Next: (a) P7b obj 4–5 — context-sync round-trip + mirror-mesh federation against the live R&D instance, now executing a RATIFIED D3; (b) operator §7.7 on ADR-011 A3 + A4 and ADR-014 A4 (held, not lapsed); (c) ⛔ **deliver the staged Hestia memo the moment her lease clears** (probe `Home.aDNA/how/sessions/active/` first; stamp the three `null` delivery fields **at** the act, then `cmp`) → her ruling → D1.3 binds with her word or gets amended; (d) the fleet host-move wave, unblocked by D5; (e) fire the 10-row repoint runbook when gates open; (f) the ungated-vault installs (`WGS`/`WilhelmAI`) + the archived-vault refuse-all disposition; (g) the tooling cascade.** **Debt register (carried; the Hestia E3 memo does NOT clear — authored, send refused by the lease probe):** **Hestia E3 delivery (staged, awaiting her lease)** · tooling cascade (incl. spec's stale `23/23` footer vs harness 31) · intake-log backfill 06-24→08-08 · F-W6-a probe fix · **fleet host-move wave (NOW UNBLOCKED — D5 ratified)** · F-S158-01 limb 4 (Operations) · the 10 wrapper repoints · 31 `FAIL_NONE` repos · `.adna/` template gate (Rosetta's) · **the 8 flip preconditions — P2/P6/P7/P8 unbuilt on Venus's lane, P5 undated on Hermes's**.

> **⛩ 2026-08-20 (seventh sitting) — "THE DENOMINATOR": the fleet's secret-gate coverage is **70/117 (60%)**, not 8/10 · ADR-011 **A4 PROPOSED** · ⭐ **VENUS'S CONCURRENCE ARRIVED MID-SESSION AND EXTENDS TO REV 4 — THE P7a GATE NOW WAITS ON OPERATOR §7.7 AND NOTHING ELSE** (`session_stanley_20260820_git_secret_gate_census`).** Operator "please read the claude.md and let's continue the campaign" → open sweep (P7a **blocked on a per-send GO in Venus's vault, verified at source — not ours to advance**) → 2-question plan gate (**scope = census + A4, repoints staged not fired** · **denominator = every push-capable repo, externals exempted-but-enumerated**) → plan approval (`please-read-the-claude-md-melodic-quilt.md`). **(1) ⭐ THE GATE MOVED WHILE WE WERE LOOKING ELSEWHERE.** Three inbound landed **mid-session** after a clean open sweep — **F-INTAKE-04's third consecutive live instance**, and this time it was load-bearing: **Venus's concurrence is delivered**, under the operator's all-sends GO, and **`concurs_with` now reads `@ revision 4 (EXTENDED S392, read at the object 847bccb)`** — §E1 extending over exactly the two clauses rev 4 moved (§D1.5a/§D1.5b + D1.3), **verified independently by her before extending** rather than taken from our memo. **Her half of the P7a exit gate is CLOSED; only operator §7.7 on ADR-015 rev 4 remains. F-P7a-g closes with it.** Two items owed back, **recorded not actioned** (outside the plan gate): **E2** — the D1.3 fallback is not merely costly but **GATED**: Regency P1 (hardware custody for a **plaintext root, in one place, on a daily-driver laptop**, `-encrypt` unretrofittable) has come up **NO-GO ×12**, and the operator's S380/S381 ruling binds CA custody explicitly ⇒ *a window budgeted for "real work on Venus's lane" is budgeted wrong if the real work is behind a gate that has not opened in twelve attempts*; lands in the **flip runbook preconditions**, her stated preference. **E3 ⚠ SUSTAINED** — D1.3 assigns trust-anchor distribution to **Hestia's** lane and **she has not seen it**; that is the **fourth** capability this ADR assigns by prose (three she caught at rev 3), so **the pattern is the finding now, not the instances**. A **Hestia memo is OWED before that clause binds** — recorded rather than sent at the tail of a session scoped elsewhere, which would repeat the same error one level up. **(2) ⛔ THE CENSUS: coverage is 60%, and the instrument was the problem three times over.** New re-runnable read-only instrument [[../how/tests/census_secret_gate|census_secret_gate.sh]] → [[what/inventory/secret_gate_census|census]] (`measured_at` 2026-08-21T02:26Z, `measured_commit` 847bccb): **124 repos** enumerated (root · nested · **worktrees**), 7 exempt **by name** → claimed population **117** → **gated 70 · ungated 47** (14 `FAIL_NOOP` + 31 `FAIL_NONE` + 2 `FAIL_LEGACY_SANITIZE`). The roster's **8/10** was measuring a ten-vault *scheduling list*. **All 14 no-ops have live remotes and push ungated today; the repair is 10 wrapper files**, because 13 of 14 reach the no-op through a symlinked wrapper (one — `Operations/what/operations-bridge` — is a **copy** with nothing to repoint). **Three of the census's own columns could not fail, and fixing each caught something:** ⛔ **`core.hooksPath` WINS** — `ScienceStanley.aDNA`'s `.git/hooks/pre-push` adjudicates **PASS** while the hook git actually runs is the **no-op**; `Archive.aDNA/lattice-labs` points `core.hooksPath` at an **absolute path outside the workspace that no longer exists**, so it runs **nothing** — and the git-lfs hook we characterised **earlier in this same session** as "occupying the slot" is **dead behind it**, a reading taken from a path git does not consult, **corrected in census §5 rather than quietly dropped**. ⚠ **That is F-Astro's exact mechanism (Wave 2, 06-22) — fixed in the vault where it was found and NEVER SWEPT FOR; the second instance was still live two months later. A finding closed at its instance is not a finding closed.** ⛔ **The enumeration predicate is part of the instrument** — `[ -d .git ]` is false for a linked worktree, so **four `latlab` worktrees**, all pushing to a live GitHub origin through one shared no-op, were **excluded outright** = rendered as absence-of-a-problem (**A3 §3 one level up**: A3 made *no gate* get a row; A4 makes *not looked at* get one). ⛔ **A closed digest table mis-reads a control that is STRONGER** — `dataroom`'s class-L refuse-all (**A3 §3 inverted**). **(3) META-CONTROL — and it found a real defect in our own instrument.** Per ADR-015 §D1.5b applied reflexively (*a guard that has only ever passed is untested*): six sabotage fixtures, each required to **fail**, plus a known-good control required to still **pass** (an instrument stuck at FAIL is as useless as one stuck at PASS). Fixture E exposed the census's `FAIL_DANGLING_SYMLINK` branch as **UNREACHABLE** (`[ -e ]` is false for a broken link ⇒ the row fell into the `absent` arm), reporting a **broken** hook as a **missing** one and sending a repairer to the wrong fix — **an unreachable branch in a checker is F-P7a-f in a different coat**, found only because the fixture existed. Fixed; all six re-verified. **(4) [[what/decisions/adr_011_secret_scanning|ADR-011 A4]] `proposed`** (extends A3; **A3's ratified text NOT edited**): §1 population ruling (the roster is a *scheduling artifact*, never a denominator; the exempt set prints **with** the number, never behind it) · §2 four new adjudication classes incl. **unknown digest → `UNCLASSIFIED` counts as FAIL** and **git-lfs slots are COMPOSED, never overwritten** · §3 **binding resolution order** + every hook-mechanism finding now carries a fleet sweep or a **dated decision not to sweep** · §4 worktrees in-population + **a shim and its target are ONE repo** (double-counting flatters coverage whenever the shared gate passes) · §5 content ≠ execution, presence ≠ content · §6 **no conformance instrument is trusted until demonstrated to fail**. **(5) Recorded plainly, against ourselves: skeleton v2 has exactly ONE live installation fleet-wide — `aDNALabs.aDNA`.** Git.aDNA, which authored and validated it, is itself on `f255e2a0…`. *"We shipped v2"* and *"v2 is deployed"* have been used interchangeably in this STATE and **they differ by 116 repos.** **(6) [[how/campaigns/campaign_git_genesis/missions/p7a_flip_staging/gate_repoint_runbook|gate_repoint_runbook]] STAGED — fires nothing**; 10 rows lowest-exposure-first, each gated per Rule 10, each retiring its caveat on an **induced positive, never an md5**. **ADR-014 A3 §1 ruled in his favour** — *"never a second standing timer"* constrains what co-dev **adds**, not whether the nightly continues ⇒ his spec §2.1 stands, the runner does not branch on mode; his `pending_declaration`-not-`steady_state` call credited as the harder discipline; **⚠ ADR-011-A4 / ADR-014-A4 naming collision flagged**) · → **Venus** (above) · → **Rosetta** (`ack_required`; `aDNA.aDNA` is `FAIL_NOOP` **through its own wrapper**, and **`.adna/` itself has NO gate** — standard-side and hers alone; release batch → **10 fixes**) · → **Galileo** (**6 ungated entry points behind 2 files**; why they were *structurally invisible*, not overlooked) · → **Hypnos** (`ack_required`; **PercySleep is I-strict** and runs the legacy sanitize hook — **a real control, not this control**, credited as such; 3 costed dispositions, compose recommended; **A1/D4 full-history scan + operator sign-off comes BEFORE any new gate** — a gate protects the future and says nothing about what is already committed) · → **fleet notice** ×7 (one-command self-check + the two checks that fail). **Findings: F-P7a-h** (*the enumeration predicate is part of the instrument — a repo it excludes is **unmeasured**, not healthy*) · **F-P7a-i** (*a hook read from a path git does not consult reports the health of a file nothing runs* — **F-Astro's second instance**, and the sweep-obligation clause it forced) · **F-P7a-j** (*a closed adjudication table mis-reads a stronger control as a failure*) · **F-P7a-k** (*content is not execution; presence is not content*) · **F-P7a-e CLOSED by measurement** · **F-P7a-g CLOSED** (Venus's memo delivered). ⏭ **Next: (a) ⭐ OPERATOR §7.7 ON ADR-015 rev 4 → P7a CLOSES (Venus's half is done); (b) operator §7.7 on ADR-011 A3 + A4 and ADR-014 A4; Her reply is **`status: staged`** in `Network.aDNA/who/coordination/`, carries **no `delivered_*` fields**, and **has never reached our tree**; her own `STATE.md:45` lists it under operator-side work (*"GO on two staged memos"*). ⇒ **The gate is blocked on a per-send operator GO in her vault, not on her.** Recorded **observed-at-source, NOT received** — the gate is **not** stamped half-closed, because treating an unsent memo as sent is the same error we have spent three sittings filing against transcribed status, wearing the opposite coat. **F-P7a-g**: fifth instance in eight days across four vaults of the staged-memo-already-readable class (her F-S390-01 · Berthier's F-S218-01 · our F-P7a-b/-d); curl `%{num_redirects}`=0 without `-L`). **A redirect is not a chain; it is a 3xx status with a `Location` header.** ⇒ **the column returned "empty" and PASSED on precisely the failure it exists to catch.** It was **not** a runbook defect: it is in **ADR-015 §D1.5a**, the ADR's *only* statement of what the probe measures, which the runbook faithfully transcribes and names as its authority — fixing the runbook alone would have left the authority unfalsifiable and any re-derivation would restore it. *(And the column entered this contract in **his own** 08-19 audit memo, adopted wholesale into rev 2 the next day: **the vault that adopts a peer's measurement owns it from that moment.**)* **(3) [[what/decisions/adr_015_lighthouse_integration_architecture|ADR-015]] → rev 4** (`proposed`; Network's `Lattice Mesh CA` (`267978…447e`, `nebula-cert`) is a **Nebula** CA and **cannot issue browser/git-client-valid TLS** ⇒ the fallback means standing up a **second CA of a different class** (key custody · rotation ceremony · anchor distribution, all her lane). the open sweep had run clean. **F-A4-01 sustained**: A4 §1 declares a conf omitting `freshness_mode` an error — but the field exists in **no conf yet** and A4 named **no transition into force**, so read literally at the ratification instant **every already-enrolled replica is an error, through no act of any owning graph**. > **🔄 Host-role inversion (ADR-013, operator-ratified 2026-06-20 mid-beachhead):** **GitHub = public home** (released FOSS) · **Codeberg = private development of FOSS-in-progress** (opened to GitHub at release; ToS-compliant) · **proprietary/client → GitHub-private → self-hosted** (unchanged). Revives ADR-003's direction, ToS-scoped. The beachhead proved **both backends live in their real roles** (GitHub-API + Forgejo-API).
